### Reconciliation principle

Do not select the more polished draft by appearance. Preserve both variants in a versioned evidence ledger:

\[
\text{claim or mechanism}
\rightarrow
\text{draft occurrence}
\rightarrow
\text{definition}
\rightarrow
\text{assumptions}
\rightarrow
\text{supporting artifact}
\rightarrow
\text{current disposition}.
\]

If two drafts use the same symbol with different meanings, they are different variables until formally reconciled. If they report different rounded outputs from the same calculation, redo the computation from stated assumptions. If no assumptions are recoverable, the result is **HISTORICAL/SUPERSEDED**, not “approximately validated.”

### Legitimate arithmetic check

The one engineering calculation specified in the research brief is reproducible under a clear assumption: fully connected layers with no bias, approximately two floating-point operations per weight for a multiply-plus-add pair.

\[
N_{\text{weights}}
=
(40 \cdot 256)
+
(256 \cdot 128)
+
(128 \cdot 64)
+
(64 \cdot 35)
\]

\[
= 10{,}240 + 32{,}768 + 8{,}192 + 2{,}240
= 53{,}440.
\]

Thus:

\[
\text{FLOPs/evaluation}
\approx 2(53{,}440)
=106{,}880.
\]

For four RK4 stages:

\[
\text{FLOPs/integration step}
\approx 4(106{,}880)
=427{,}520.
\]

At 20 integration steps per second:

\[
427{,}520 \times 20
=8{,}550{,}400
\approx 8.55\ \text{MFLOPs/s}.
\]

This arithmetic is correct **only** under the stated narrow assumptions. It excludes biases, activations, normalization, memory movement, batching, control flow, precision effects, RK4 bookkeeping beyond the model evaluations, and platform-specific overhead. It supports neither a performance claim nor an energy-efficiency claim by itself. ### Modern CA boundary

A minimal CA interface should own only the decisions no other listed subsystem owns:

\[
\text{CA} =
\text{select cognitive policy}
+
\text{allocate bounded cognitive resources}
+
\text{request learning/evaluation modes}
+
\text{coordinate cross-subsystem contracts}.
\]

CA should **not** own:

- Candidate-world storage, field realization, coalition mechanics, or activation decay: HTCE and ephemeral HVD.
- Constitutional admissibility or execution permission: HIGC.
- Path-dependent dynamics, hysteresis, or history-sensitive transition rules: THDE.
- Homeostatic observation, epistemic tracking, or causal adequacy assessment: Cyrae.
- Transformation algebra: HTLA/HTNLA.
- Logical database semantics, physical realization, trajectories, copy-on-write behavior, or durable persistence: HVD, TMS, HDF5.
- Continuity across representation/substrate changes: QMT.
- Identity continuity or identity-event history: Identity Architecture.
- External wording, rendering, and actuation projection: Maera. [ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/10268398/d3e53a42-5511-4616-a168-6d68b6726431/paste.txt?AWSAccessKeyId=ASIA2F3EMEYEX6W76NXS&Signature=vw6IUIeBHq77LfDPJWc%2FiTvMcek%3D&x-amz-security-token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEPL%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2FwEaCXVzLWVhc3QtMSJHMEUCIQD3xlh8YVV3yq2AIPUQLOgTHhNDOhZmSIi4eh3ZnBu16AIgQyM2p6upxtVoNU%2FS1UAkiO7VsL5bA0edV%2FhWh24m9uwq%2FAQIuv%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2FARABGgw2OTk3NTMzMDk3MDUiDOcmhRM27WVYqJjsDSrQBIWcLE%2BTjk9%2BQR6kj6WMMbRUemqfs6aqWXSsogG7Zhm82hZg4pLItPWNTOg4q88C3D6kTHsVcOEmsgq0yGH9wtWej8emi0OQhX95Zd8SMbYffsMsdZCbNP0OL6Cs2BHsFepQDvYO9RipVKkLVLNs8S8KfvdPm9ZbTgvMbaGr7bmji4eVn7flxbIxhFhCF8Oug20hFYnj02hFe2rI9myRdYeNYojHCUvfF0%2BNLLeWojYVxVNjrJUomlTxOI7rTxpM1S3OMtF1JMNv8GCPo6%2FiI2dZLkctmgXST8KRAVANiJcVRAc%2FNb9lKQjUXgXrLVLvSmDUhZ8b1ku6SG1QykAWw19eW6LbMvOwUcWIgaO9Fb9LMaHfZvPHgoREjSPXlXIeYYDQu9QfJDT%2BC98W359l%2F5bVLFAiAbOG8C%2BWnqVwOfulMt%2BhBGmnCL5knRa9ODpMpZBFBegDH%2B9ANlwHI2ut%2BLnnm4QScxQeOUY1elYsE7YtIm1zaF0FnVVHuMaIanagbLGPp15LGk%2Fi%2F0C9QlN9RlQtJ0h5IzhSafs4E%2Bx3FXAGa02aVYdeAFvO%2FC%2Bl1w%2FSP4se1R8eh4of22kOb5JTxr4auwE%2FSIK4X4ptstxo4D113CNPgOjKttOF9XdoaHjZtTAateYtVMnQ0pWXZHfEAQD%2BL8Kax1QKan%2BZnWRgFR%2BoGFMazPaqPia85VDyDgxR5xbDlGsr%2FHT96%2BlXkqtiiWsVgEUrBgSr%2BaboeC%2FLoTSk13hnoXEFucaOXpg%2BJnU8L7%2FuY8IBWco4gWnLuhwxJusw1vHd1AY6mAHHiUcV5x%2B0HP16yyFDUlgnMiSXa%2F6FGmTYlzat1ZBjIqH6Y5mRxRS1XW7Xk3LOCa4Qf75BbHPHz%2BcH4s5xSIlEEKwZlcUi9cObh78dGKviCazr0SppSF%2FnjyJdzHirERBE4WWBmxsPVYIt2XszNpM3DP5R0ww7g06uaopjMTVHK9njDJSAEC6It%2Bjd4ay2iqk49dsmvGFDjQ%3D%3D&Expires=1788315305)

The strongest nonredundancy requirement is this: CA chooses **how** the system should search or assess; HTCE and related infrastructure determine **where and through what state machinery** that search occurs.

## Scientific and mathematical audit

### Reward prediction error and modulation

Schultz, Dayan, and Montague’s 1997 account connected dopamine-neuron activity with deviations between predicted and obtained reward timing and magnitude, using temporal-difference learning as a computational interpretation. It is influential prior art for a **functional analogy**, not a license to name any generic scalar “dopamine” or “RPE.” [science](https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.275.5306.1593)

A CA signal may be termed a computational reward-prediction error only when the system can establish at least:

\[
\epsilon_t
=
r_{t+\Delta}
+
\gamma V(s_{t+\Delta})
-
V(s_t)
\]

with explicit meanings and provenance for:

- A predecessor state or observation \(s_t\).
- A committed pre-action prediction \(V(s_t)\), recorded before the outcome.
- An action or intervention that binds the transition.
- A post-action observation and reward/outcome measure.
- A specified temporal horizon \(\Delta\) and discount convention \(\gamma\).
- Episode identity, evidence provenance, and a causal-adequacy determination.

If these requirements are unavailable, the correct value is:

\[
\operatorname{RPEStatus}=\text{UNAVAILABLE},
\]

not \(\epsilon_t=0\). Zero says a well-defined error was evaluated and found absent; unavailable says the necessary relation was not established. This is a necessary epistemic distinction in any learning system, independent of whether it uses biological terminology. The brief assigns causal/epistemic assessment to Cyrae, so CA should request a typed signal rather than reconstruct causal validity itself. [ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/10268398/d3e53a42-5511-4616-a168-6d68b6726431/paste.txt?AWSAccessKeyId=ASIA2F3EMEYEX6W76NXS&Signature=vw6IUIeBHq77LfDPJWc%2FiTvMcek%3D&x-amz-security-token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEPL%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2FwEaCXVzLWVhc3QtMSJHMEUCIQD3xlh8YVV3yq2AIPUQLOgTHhNDOhZmSIi4eh3ZnBu16AIgQyM2p6upxtVoNU%2FS1UAkiO7VsL5bA0edV%2FhWh24m9uwq%2FAQIuv%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2FARABGgw2OTk3NTMzMDk3MDUiDOcmhRM27WVYqJjsDSrQBIWcLE%2BTjk9%2BQR6kj6WMMbRUemqfs6aqWXSsogG7Zhm82hZg4pLItPWNTOg4q88C3D6kTHsVcOEmsgq0yGH9wtWej8emi0OQhX95Zd8SMbYffsMsdZCbNP0OL6Cs2BHsFepQDvYO9RipVKkLVLNs8S8KfvdPm9ZbTgvMbaGr7bmji4eVn7flxbIxhFhCF8Oug20hFYnj02hFe2rI9myRdYeNYojHCUvfF0%2BNLLeWojYVxVNjrJUomlTxOI7rTxpM1S3OMtF1JMNv8GCPo6%2FiI2dZLkctmgXST8KRAVANiJcVRAc%2FNb9lKQjUXgXrLVLvSmDUhZ8b1ku6SG1QykAWw19eW6LbMvOwUcWIgaO9Fb9LMaHfZvPHgoREjSPXlXIeYYDQu9QfJDT%2BC98W359l%2F5bVLFAiAbOG8C%2BWnqVwOfulMt%2BhBGmnCL5knRa9ODpMpZBFBegDH%2B9ANlwHI2ut%2BLnnm4QScxQeOUY1elYsE7YtIm1zaF0FnVVHuMaIanagbLGPp15LGk%2Fi%2F0C9QlN9RlQtJ0h5IzhSafs4E%2Bx3FXAGa02aVYdeAFvO%2FC%2Bl1w%2FSP4se1R8eh4of22kOb5JTxr4auwE%2FSIK4X4ptstxo4D113CNPgOjKttOF9XdoaHjZtTAateYtVMnQ0pWXZHfEAQD%2BL8Kax1QKan%2BZnWRgFR%2BoGFMazPaqPia85VDyDgxR5xbDlGsr%2FHT96%2BlXkqtiiWsVgEUrBgSr%2BaboeC%2FLoTSk13hnoXEFucaOXpg%2BJnU8L7%2FuY8IBWco4gWnLuhwxJusw1vHd1AY6mAHHiUcV5x%2B0HP16yyFDUlgnMiSXa%2F6FGmTYlzat1ZBjIqH6Y5mRxRS1XW7Xk3LOCa4Qf75BbHPHz%2BcH4s5xSIlEEKwZlcUi9cObh78dGKviCazr0SppSF%2FnjyJdzHirERBE4WWBmxsPVYIt2XszNpM3DP5R0ww7g06uaopjMTVHK9njDJSAEC6It%2Bjd4ay2iqk49dsmvGFDjQ%3D%3D&Expires=1788315305)

**Disposition:** generic modulator — **PROPOSED**; dopamine-inspired profile — **EXPERIMENTAL**; literal dopamine concentration — **HISTORICAL/SUPERSEDED** unless the system includes a justified biological simulation.

### Global workspace

Global Workspace Theory concerns broad integration and broadcast across specialized modules. In AI-oriented discussions, a global latent workspace is proposed as an amodal shared latent space, while shared-workspace architectures implement bandwidth-limited coordination in which modules compete for access. Goyal and colleagues argue that limited shared capacity can encourage specialization, compositionality, and synchronization. [pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/34001376/)

This supports the following relationship:

| CA workspace proposition | Relationship to prior art | Modern Nexus interpretation |
|---|---|---|
| Modules compete for limited cognitive availability | RELATED PRIOR ART | HTCE coalition/field competition |
| Selected content becomes broadly available | FUNCTIONAL ANALOGUE | HTCE active-field realization and addressability |
| CA regulates attention or workspace policy | COMPATIBLE INTERPRETATION | CA may configure eligibility, resource policy, or regime |
| Workspace implies consciousness | NO MEANINGFUL INFERENCE | Functional architecture does not establish phenomenology |

Therefore, avoid a competing CA “workspace.” CA can set workspace policy; HTCE should own the active field and coalition realization. ### Variational objectives and active inference

The prompt calls for testing whether a legacy variational objective relates to Friston’s free-energy framework, but no legacy objective has been supplied. No direct mathematical conclusion is currently possible.

The admissible classifications are:

- **DIRECT MATHEMATICAL RELATION:** only if the actual CA objective can be mapped term-by-term to a variational bound on model evidence or a specified expected-free-energy objective.
- **COMPATIBLE INTERPRETATION:** if CA maintains predictive beliefs or optimizes uncertainty-sensitive policy without proving that its objective is the active-inference objective.
- **FUNCTIONAL ANALOGUE:** if CA samples or selects informative candidates.
- **WEAK ANALOGY:** if it merely uses terms such as prediction, surprise, or uncertainty.
- **NO MEANINGFUL RELATION:** if no generative model, variational posterior, or identifiable objective exists.

Do not call a general GAN objective, prediction residual, entropy term, or exploration bonus “free energy” without this mapping. The attachment properly warns against forcing all CA dynamics into active inference. ### STDP and temporally dependent learning

Bi and Poo’s foundational experiment demonstrated that synaptic modification depended on the relative timing of presynaptic and postsynaptic spikes: in their cultured-neuron experiment, timing within roughly 20 ms after presynaptic activation was associated with potentiation, while the reverse ordering was associated with depression. That result makes event timing—not merely a standalone kernel—the central object in a genuine STDP interpretation. [pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/9852584/)

For the legacy expression:

\[
\operatorname{HebbianUpdate}(A,B)
=
\int
\left[
D1_{\rm act}\max(K(\tau),0)
+
D2_{\rm act}\min(K(\tau),0)
\right]
(A\circ B)\,d\tau,
\]

if \(A\circ B\) is independent of \(\tau\), then:

\[
\operatorname{HebbianUpdate}(A,B)
=
\left(
\int
D1_{\rm act}\max(K(\tau),0)
+
D2_{\rm act}\min(K(\tau),0)
\,d\tau
\right)
(A\circ B).
\]

Under constant \(D1_{\rm act}\) and \(D2_{\rm act}\), this is simply:

\[
\operatorname{HebbianUpdate}(A,B)
=
\kappa(A\circ B),
\]

for scalar \(\kappa\). It is a modulated correlation update, but not inherently timing-dependent learning. This is a mathematical conclusion conditional on the prompt’s description; inspect the original equation before finalizing it. [ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/10268398/d3e53a42-5511-4616-a168-6d68b6726431/paste.txt?AWSAccessKeyId=ASIA2F3EMEYEX6W76NXS&Signature=vw6IUIeBHq77LfDPJWc%2FiTvMcek%3D&x-amz-security-token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEPL%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2FwEaCXVzLWVhc3QtMSJHMEUCIQD3xlh8YVV3yq2AIPUQLOgTHhNDOhZmSIi4eh3ZnBu16AIgQyM2p6upxtVoNU%2FS1UAkiO7VsL5bA0edV%2FhWh24m9uwq%2FAQIuv%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2FARABGgw2OTk3NTMzMDk3MDUiDOcmhRM27WVYqJjsDSrQBIWcLE%2BTjk9%2BQR6kj6WMMbRUemqfs6aqWXSsogG7Zhm82hZg4pLItPWNTOg4q88C3D6kTHsVcOEmsgq0yGH9wtWej8emi0OQhX95Zd8SMbYffsMsdZCbNP0OL6Cs2BHsFepQDvYO9RipVKkLVLNs8S8KfvdPm9ZbTgvMbaGr7bmji4eVn7flxbIxhFhCF8Oug20hFYnj02hFe2rI9myRdYeNYojHCUvfF0%2BNLLeWojYVxVNjrJUomlTxOI7rTxpM1S3OMtF1JMNv8GCPo6%2FiI2dZLkctmgXST8KRAVANiJcVRAc%2FNb9lKQjUXgXrLVLvSmDUhZ8b1ku6SG1QykAWw19eW6LbMvOwUcWIgaO9Fb9LMaHfZvPHgoREjSPXlXIeYYDQu9QfJDT%2BC98W359l%2F5bVLFAiAbOG8C%2BWnqVwOfulMt%2BhBGmnCL5knRa9ODpMpZBFBegDH%2B9ANlwHI2ut%2BLnnm4QScxQeOUY1elYsE7YtIm1zaF0FnVVHuMaIanagbLGPp15LGk%2Fi%2F0C9QlN9RlQtJ0h5IzhSafs4E%2Bx3FXAGa02aVYdeAFvO%2FC%2Bl1w%2FSP4se1R8eh4of22kOb5JTxr4auwE%2FSIK4X4ptstxo4D113CNPgOjKttOF9XdoaHjZtTAateYtVMnQ0pWXZHfEAQD%2BL8Kax1QKan%2BZnWRgFR%2BoGFMazPaqPia85VDyDgxR5xbDlGsr%2FHT96%2BlXkqtiiWsVgEUrBgSr%2BaboeC%2FLoTSk13hnoXEFucaOXpg%2BJnU8L7%2FuY8IBWco4gWnLuhwxJusw1vHd1AY6mAHHiUcV5x%2B0HP16yyFDUlgnMiSXa%2F6FGmTYlzat1ZBjIqH6Y5mRxRS1XW7Xk3LOCa4Qf75BbHPHz%2BcH4s5xSIlEEKwZlcUi9cObh78dGKviCazr0SppSF%2FnjyJdzHirERBE4WWBmxsPVYIt2XszNpM3DP5R0ww7g06uaopjMTVHK9njDJSAEC6It%2Bjd4ay2iqk49dsmvGFDjQ%3D%3D&Expires=1788315305)

A valid computational analogue requires observable event pairs:

\[
\Delta t = t_{\rm post}-t_{\rm pre},
\]

and a declared update rule such as:

\[
\Delta W_{ij}
=
\sum_{(e_i,e_j)}
g(\Delta t_{ij}, z_t)\,\psi_i\,\psi_j,
\]

where \(e_i,e_j\) are timestamped events, \(z_t\) is a bounded computational modulation vector, and \(\psi\) defines the represented activity. If Nexus lacks meaningful pre/post events at that layer, retire “STDP” and use **temporally modulated, history-dependent adaptation** instead.

### The alleged \(\Phi\) metric

For random variables \(X_1,\ldots,X_n\), the proposed score is:

\[
S(X)
=
\sum_i I(X_i;X_{-i}).
\]

It computes the sum of each component’s mutual information with the rest of the system. It is **not** standard IIT \(\Phi\), which is defined through more specific causal/integrated-information constructions and partition comparisons. It is also not generally equal to total correlation, multi-information, dual total correlation, or binding information.

For example, total correlation is:

\[
\operatorname{TC}(X)
=
\sum_i H(X_i)-H(X_1,\ldots,X_n),
\]

while dual total correlation has a different entropy decomposition. The proposed \(S(X)\) double-counts shared dependence in ways that differ from both. Thus its proper provisional name is something descriptive, such as:

\[
\text{aggregate component–rest dependence},
\]

not “IIT \(\Phi\).”

No threshold—whether \(\Phi>0.5\) or any other number—can infer consciousness. Recent consciousness-science work underscores why: AI-consciousness assessments are framed as theory-dependent indicators that update credences, not as a scalar detector; an adversarial collaboration tested predictions of Global Neuronal Workspace and Integrated Information theories rather than treating either as settled. [arxiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.08708)

**Disposition:** dependency metric — **EXPERIMENTAL** only if it predicts a specified operational outcome; IIT-\(\Phi\) claim — **HISTORICAL/SUPERSEDED**; consciousness threshold — **HISTORICAL/SUPERSEDED**.

### Sleep, dreaming, replay, and perturbation

The literature gives real but limited antecedents:

- Synaptic Homeostasis Hypothesis proposes that sleep serves synaptic renormalization after wake-related plasticity. Frank’s critique argues that underlying mechanisms remain insufficiently defined. This supports a research question about normalization and continual-learning stability, not a conclusion that a Nexus perturbation procedure is sleep-like or correct. [pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC3583075/)
- Hobson, Hong, and Friston present dreaming as a generative or predictive-model account under altered sensory conditions. It is a theoretical neuroscience proposal—not evidence that a software system’s offline mode is dreaming or conscious. [pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/25346710/)
- Replay and generative replay have computational support as mechanisms for memory consolidation and stabilizing learning in artificial networks. This provides stronger engineering grounding for structured replay than for uninformed random perturbation. [pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC9758580/)

The recommended order of preference is:

\[
\text{retrieval-conditioned replay}
\succ
\text{learned world-model sampling}
\succ
\text{structured counterfactual recombination}
\succ
\text{bounded stochastic perturbation}
\succ
\text{unbounded noise}.
\]

That is a design hypothesis, not a universal law. Compare them experimentally under identical resource budgets and no-promotion safeguards.

## Counterfactual cognition and regimes

### Green-sky architecture

The green-sky test describes a standard counterfactual-scoping requirement: reason from a local premise without either rejecting it for contradicting current canonical belief or mutating canonical belief merely by considering it. The requested Nexus decomposition supports the following design:

\[
\text{CA regime/policy}
\rightarrow
\text{HTCE candidate field}
\rightarrow
\text{ephemeral HVD}
\rightarrow
\text{HTLA/HTNLA transformations}
\rightarrow
\text{TMS realization}.
\]

CA should issue a request such as:

\[
\operatorname{OpenHypotheticalField}
(
\text{assumption}=\texttt{sky=green},
\text{scope}=w,
\text{lifetime}=L,
\text{authority}=\texttt{noncanonical},
\text{promotion}=\texttt{explicit-only}
).
\]

The field must carry at least:

| Metadata | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `field_id` | Unique scope and audit identity |
| `world_scope` | Defines the local hypothetical world |
| `epistemic_scope` | Marks assumption, evidence, uncertainty, and contradiction state |
| `provenance` | Records origin, generator, source material, and transformations |
| `canonical_status` | Explicitly noncanonical by default |
| `creation_reason` | Distinguishes planning, fiction, diagnosis, simulation, or research |
| `lifetime` | Defines expiration/decay conditions |
| `mutation_authority` | Prevents writes to canonical memory |
| `promotion_criteria` | Requires evidence, review, and explicit authorized transition |
| `actuation_authority` | Must be absent unless separately granted through proper gates |

This makes “truth within hypothesis” a scoped proposition:

\[
\operatorname{True}(p \mid w)
\not\Rightarrow
\operatorname{CanonicalTrue}(p).
\]

The correct safety rule is:

\[
\text{candidate weirdness}
\neq
\text{candidate inadmissibility}.
\]

A creative field may relax selected world-model assumptions or prior preferences. It must not relax constitutional invariants, identity protection, provenance, authority boundaries, or actuation controls. Candidate generation, candidate selection, and candidate enactment must remain separate transitions. ### Does CA need a regime vector?

The answer is currently **UNRESOLVED**. There are three valid outcomes, exactly as the research brief anticipates:

| Outcome | Condition | CA consequence |
|---|---|---|
| Substantial controller | A low-dimensional state produces measurable, transferable, controllable benefits beyond field-local controls | CA owns a bounded regime controller |
| Thin policy vector | Benefits occur, but only as configuration of existing HTCE/TUCR/Cyrae/THDE interfaces | CA exposes policy parameters, not new cognitive machinery |
| No separate modulation layer | Existing field-local and runtime mechanisms reproduce all effects | Retire modulation state; minimize CA |

A generic nonbiological formulation is:

\[
x_{t+1}=F(x_t,e_t,M_t,m_t)
\]

\[
m_{t+1}=G(m_t,x_t,e_t,h_t,u_t),
\]

where \(x_t\) is semantic/cognitive state and \(m_t\) is a bounded regime-policy state. These are design sketches, not canonical equations. If retained, dimensions should be generic and behaviorally defined rather than named after neurotransmitters:

\[
m_t =
(
b_{\rm association},
b_{\rm diversity},
b_{\rm novelty},
b_{\rm exploration},
b_{\rm counterfactual},
b_{\rm prior\ rigidity},
b_{\rm sensory\ anchor},
b_{\rm persistence},
b_{\rm scrutiny},
b_{\rm resource}
).
\]

Optional dopamine-, norepinephrine-, serotonin-, acetylcholine-, glutamate-, or GABA-inspired parameterizations can be experimental profiles, but the base architecture should not represent literal neurochemistry without a biological-modeling purpose and evidence. The modern neuroscience literature motivates the broad idea that neural modulation relates to learning, attention, uncertainty, and behavioral flexibility; it does not validate six arbitrary software coordinates or fixed software thresholds as biological quantities. [science](https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.275.5306.1593)

### Regimes as testable policies

Avoid reifying anthropomorphic labels. A regime is valid only if it has operational controls, measurable outputs, and a falsifiable benefit.

| Candidate regime | Operational policy differences | Required outcomes |
|---|---|---|
| Critical / analytic | Strong evidence and provenance weighting; narrower candidate acceptance; high contradiction testing | Better calibration, factual accuracy, and detection of invalid inferences |
| Creative / divergent | Broader association radius; higher diversity; reduced prior filtering within noncanonical scope | More valid novel candidates without higher canonical contamination |
| Abstract | More relational/structural features; reduced dependence on immediate sensory particulars | Better transfer across surface forms or domains |
| Daydream-like | Internally initiated field expansion; reduced sensory anchoring; episodic recombination | Better useful recombination versus matched compute baselines |
| Offline dream-like | Sensory attenuation plus replay or learned counterfactual sampling | Better retention, model learning, planning, or robust exploration |

“Creative” does not mean lower safety or actuation standards. It means a different search policy in a noncanonical field.

### Typed epistemic value

The proposal that information-seeking value should depend on **the kind** of epistemic deficiency, not merely a scalar uncertainty, is a worthwhile **RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS**. The prompt’s distinctions are structurally meaningful:

- **AMBIGUOUS:** multiple admissible hypotheses exist; discriminating evidence may be valuable.
- **UNKNOWN:** a needed relation, model, or evidence source is unavailable; additional sensing may not resolve it unless the missing prerequisite becomes available.
- **IMPOSSIBLE:** no admissible hypothesis satisfies current evidence/model; the appropriate action may be model repair, provenance review, or constraint diagnosis—not ordinary exploration.
- **EXACT:** a singleton possibility set under the validated relation.

A cautious policy form is:

\[
\operatorname{EVI}(a \mid z)
=
\mathbb E[
U_{\rm epistemic}(q_{t+1}) - U_{\rm epistemic}(q_t)
\mid a,z
]
-
C(a),
\]

where \(z\) is a typed epistemic status and \(q_t\) includes the possibility relation, not merely entropy. The model must defer to finalized HTLA semantics and must not claim that the four labels form an independent lattice; only the powerset of a well-defined possibility space is guaranteed to form a lattice under inclusion. A decisive experiment compares a type-aware information policy with a scalar-entropy policy. The hypothesis fails if typed status produces no improvement in evidence efficiency, calibration, safety, or recovery from impossible/unknown states.

## Sparsity, claims, and experiments

### The 70/30 lineage

The historical provenance is stipulated: CA’s 30%-active / 70%-inactive policy was intentionally inherited by later HSL/HTLA work. That is **CANONICAL design-history ground truth within the provided prompt**, not evidence that the ratio is biologically privileged, mathematically optimal, or applicable at all levels. [ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/10268398/d3e53a42-5511-4616-a168-6d68b6726431/paste.txt?AWSAccessKeyId=ASIA2F3EMEYEX6W76NXS&Signature=vw6IUIeBHq77LfDPJWc%2FiTvMcek%3D&x-amz-security-token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEPL%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2FwEaCXVzLWVhc3QtMSJHMEUCIQD3xlh8YVV3yq2AIPUQLOgTHhNDOhZmSIi4eh3ZnBu16AIgQyM2p6upxtVoNU%2FS1UAkiO7VsL5bA0edV%2FhWh24m9uwq%2FAQIuv%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2FARABGgw2OTk3NTMzMDk3MDUiDOcmhRM27WVYqJjsDSrQBIWcLE%2BTjk9%2BQR6kj6WMMbRUemqfs6aqWXSsogG7Zhm82hZg4pLItPWNTOg4q88C3D6kTHsVcOEmsgq0yGH9wtWej8emi0OQhX95Zd8SMbYffsMsdZCbNP0OL6Cs2BHsFepQDvYO9RipVKkLVLNs8S8KfvdPm9ZbTgvMbaGr7bmji4eVn7flxbIxhFhCF8Oug20hFYnj02hFe2rI9myRdYeNYojHCUvfF0%2BNLLeWojYVxVNjrJUomlTxOI7rTxpM1S3OMtF1JMNv8GCPo6%2FiI2dZLkctmgXST8KRAVANiJcVRAc%2FNb9lKQjUXgXrLVLvSmDUhZ8b1ku6SG1QykAWw19eW6LbMvOwUcWIgaO9Fb9LMaHfZvPHgoREjSPXlXIeYYDQu9QfJDT%2BC98W359l%2F5bVLFAiAbOG8C%2BWnqVwOfulMt%2BhBGmnCL5knRa9ODpMpZBFBegDH%2B9ANlwHI2ut%2BLnnm4QScxQeOUY1elYsE7YtIm1zaF0FnVVHuMaIanagbLGPp15LGk%2Fi%2F0C9QlN9RlQtJ0h5IzhSafs4E%2Bx3FXAGa02aVYdeAFvO%2FC%2Bl1w%2FSP4se1R8eh4of22kOb5JTxr4auwE%2FSIK4X4ptstxo4D113CNPgOjKttOF9XdoaHjZtTAateYtVMnQ0pWXZHfEAQD%2BL8Kax1QKan%2BZnWRgFR%2BoGFMazPaqPia85VDyDgxR5xbDlGsr%2FHT96%2BlXkqtiiWsVgEUrBgSr%2BaboeC%2FLoTSk13hnoXEFucaOXpg%2BJnU8L7%2FuY8IBWco4gWnLuhwxJusw1vHd1AY6mAHHiUcV5x%2B0HP16yyFDUlgnMiSXa%2F6FGmTYlzat1ZBjIqH6Y5mRxRS1XW7Xk3LOCa4Qf75BbHPHz%2BcH4s5xSIlEEKwZlcUi9cObh78dGKviCazr0SppSF%2FnjyJdzHirERBE4WWBmxsPVYIt2XszNpM3DP5R0ww7g06uaopjMTVHK9njDJSAEC6It%2Bjd4ay2iqk49dsmvGFDjQ%3D%3D&Expires=1788315305)

The central correction is:

\[
\rho_{\text{active,cognition}}
\neq
\rho_{\text{active,HSL-coordinate}}.
\]

They denote different phenomena:

| Quantity | Example |
|---|---|
| Cognitive sparsity | Fraction of candidate fields, modules, thoughts, or coalitions active |
| Representational sparsity | Fraction of nonzero or selected HSL coordinates |
| Computational sparsity | Fraction of arithmetic/memory access avoided |
| Field sparsity | Number of live ephemeral HVD worlds |
| Connectivity sparsity | Fraction of active couplings |

A sparse result need not use sparse computation; a broad candidate population can contain sparse representations; a focused candidate set can use dense representations. Thus the historical inheritance should be recorded, but the coupling must be treated as **RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS**, not carried forward automatically. ### Resource language

The legacy values “85% resource/energy ceiling,” “70% sparse activation,” and “42% energy reduction” are **UNVERIFIED** unless accompanied by unit definitions, instrumentation, baselines, hardware, workload, and reproducible measurements. A legitimate engineering constraint is:

\[
R(t)\leq R_{\max},
\]

where \(R\) may represent a declared compute, latency, memory, power, or financial-resource budget. It must not be called metabolic or thermodynamic energy unless physical quantities and measurements warrant that claim. [ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/10268398/d3e53a42-5511-4616-a168-6d68b6726431/paste.txt?AWSAccessKeyId=ASIA2F3EMEYEX6W76NXS&Signature=vw6IUIeBHq77LfDPJWc%2FiTvMcek%3D&x-amz-security-token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEPL%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2FwEaCXVzLWVhc3QtMSJHMEUCIQD3xlh8YVV3yq2AIPUQLOgTHhNDOhZmSIi4eh3ZnBu16AIgQyM2p6upxtVoNU%2FS1UAkiO7VsL5bA0edV%2FhWh24m9uwq%2FAQIuv%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2F%2FARABGgw2OTk3NTMzMDk3MDUiDOcmhRM27WVYqJjsDSrQBIWcLE%2BTjk9%2BQR6kj6WMMbRUemqfs6aqWXSsogG7Zhm82hZg4pLItPWNTOg4q88C3D6kTHsVcOEmsgq0yGH9wtWej8emi0OQhX95Zd8SMbYffsMsdZCbNP0OL6Cs2BHsFepQDvYO9RipVKkLVLNs8S8KfvdPm9ZbTgvMbaGr7bmji4eVn7flxbIxhFhCF8Oug20hFYnj02hFe2rI9myRdYeNYojHCUvfF0%2BNLLeWojYVxVNjrJUomlTxOI7rTxpM1S3OMtF1JMNv8GCPo6%2FiI2dZLkctmgXST8KRAVANiJcVRAc%2FNb9lKQjUXgXrLVLvSmDUhZ8b1ku6SG1QykAWw19eW6LbMvOwUcWIgaO9Fb9LMaHfZvPHgoREjSPXlXIeYYDQu9QfJDT%2BC98W359l%2F5bVLFAiAbOG8C%2BWnqVwOfulMt%2BhBGmnCL5knRa9ODpMpZBFBegDH%2B9ANlwHI2ut%2BLnnm4QScxQeOUY1elYsE7YtIm1zaF0FnVVHuMaIanagbLGPp15LGk%2Fi%2F0C9QlN9RlQtJ0h5IzhSafs4E%2Bx3FXAGa02aVYdeAFvO%2FC%2Bl1w%2FSP4se1R8eh4of22kOb5JTxr4auwE%2FSIK4X4ptstxo4D113CNPgOjKttOF9XdoaHjZtTAateYtVMnQ0pWXZHfEAQD%2BL8Kax1QKan%2BZnWRgFR%2BoGFMazPaqPia85VDyDgxR5xbDlGsr%2FHT96%2BlXkqtiiWsVgEUrBgSr%2BaboeC%2FLoTSk13hnoXEFucaOXpg%2BJnU8L7%2FuY8IBWco4gWnLuhwxJusw1vHd1AY6mAHHiUcV5x%2B0HP16yyFDUlgnMiSXa%2F6FGmTYlzat1ZBjIqH6Y5mRxRS1XW7Xk3LOCa4Qf75BbHPHz%2BcH4s5xSIlEEKwZlcUi9cObh78dGKviCazr0SppSF%2FnjyJdzHirERBE4WWBmxsPVYIt2XszNpM3DP5R0ww7g06uaopjMTVHK9njDJSAEC6It%2Bjd4ay2iqk49dsmvGFDjQ%3D%3D&Expires=1788315305)

### Required experiment program

| Experiment | Compare | Primary measures | Falsification criterion |
|---|---|---|---|
| Modulation ablation | HTCE alone; field-local controls; CA thin policy; CA low-dimensional state | Diversity, calibration, task-switching, counterfactual depth, stability, resource cost | Remove CA modulation if it yields no reproducible incremental benefit |
| Green-sky isolation | Scoped hypothetical fields vs ordinary canonical state | Recursive inference quality, canonical-state integrity, audit completeness | Reject design if hypothetical content can mutate canonical history without explicit authorized promotion |
| Provenance penetration | Adversarially inject imagined, retrieved, and external claims | False promotion rate, trace completeness, recovery latency | Reject if provenance/scope boundary is bypassable |
| Offline replay | No replay; random noise; structured perturbation; retrieval replay; learned generative sampling | Retention, interference, world-model accuracy, planning, diversity | Retire any offline mechanism dominated by equal-budget baseline |
| Sparsity policy | Fixed 30%; adaptive; regime-conditioned; resource-conditioned | Accuracy, latency, compute, stability, retrieval, creative breadth, critical selectivity | Retire fixed 30% as universal policy if adaptive policies reliably dominate |
| HSL coupling | Independent cognitive and coordinate sparsity vs coupled 30/30 policy | Task performance, compute, representational quality, robustness | Decouple if coupling provides no independent benefit |
| Integration metric | Retain, shuffle, and alternative dependence metrics | Prediction of specified task/coordination outcomes | Remove metric if it lacks predictive utility or stability |
| Biological terminology | Generic modulation vs six named neurotransmitter coordinates | Interpretability, control, transfer, engineering complexity | Use generic terms if biological labels add no testable value |

### Claim-status table

| Proposition | Status | Required condition |
|---|---|---|
| CA historically preceded the 30/70 policy inherited by HSL/HTLA | CANONICAL | Supplied as explicit design-history ground truth in the brief |
| Nexus has demonstrated phenomenal consciousness | HISTORICAL/SUPERSEDED | Must not be claimed |
| CA is a generic orchestration/regime-policy layer | PROPOSED | Needs formal contracts and ablation evidence |
| HTCE realizes candidate fields and coalitions | INFERRED | Based on prompt; verify against authoritative HTCE specification |
| CA regulates workspace policy while HTCE realizes workspace field | PROPOSED | Validate nonredundancy and interface design |
| Generic RPE consumption requires causal provenance | PROPOSED | Formalize Nexus causal contract; test unavailable vs zero handling |
| Dopamine-inspired profile | EXPERIMENTAL | Must stay distinct from biological dopamine |
| D1/D2 thresholds \(0.5,0.3\) are biologically derived | HISTORICAL/SUPERSEDED | No supporting evidence supplied |
| Legacy STDP equation is genuine STDP | UNRESOLVED | Depends on actual timing variables and event semantics |
| \(\sum_i I(X_i;X_{-i})\) is IIT-\(\Phi\) | HISTORICAL/SUPERSEDED | It is not standard IIT \(\Phi\) |
| Aggregate dependence metric may be useful | EXPERIMENTAL | Must predict an operational outcome |
| Dream-like offline mode improves learning | RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS | Compare against matched baselines |
| Learned generative sampling beats blind perturbation | RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS | Test under equal resources and safety boundaries |
| Fixed 30% activity is universally optimal | HISTORICAL/SUPERSEDED | No derivation or broad evidence supplied |
| Dynamic sparsity is superior | RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS | Must outperform fixed policies |
| Cognitive sparsity should constrain HSL occupancy | RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS | Must demonstrate benefit beyond accidental historical inheritance |
| Green-sky reasoning requires a new CA imagination subsystem | UNRESOLVED | Test whether HTCE + ephemeral HVD already suffice |
| Candidate weirdness is not inadmissibility | PROPOSED | Must coexist with hard constitutional and authority gates |

## Proposed CA paper structure

Do not yet publish a canonical replacement paper. First recover the original source corpus and run the experimental program. The eventual document should be organized as follows:

1. **Scope, status, and nonclaims**
   Claim-status vocabulary; no demonstrated phenomenal consciousness; computational vs functional-analogue vs biological claims.

2. **Founding-document archaeology**
   Version map, duplicate reconciliation, provenance ledger, withdrawn claims, and preserved intents.

3. **Modern architectural boundary**
   CA’s narrow ownership; explicit nonownership; contracts with HTCE, HIGC, THDE, Cyrae, HTLA/HTNLA, HVD/TMS/HDF5, QMT, Identity Architecture, TUCR, and Maera.

4. **Cognitive-policy model**
   Candidate regime variables, allowed controls, resource policies, typed RPE interface, and field-opening interface.

5. **Counterfactual cognition**
   Scoped hypothetical acceptance, green-sky test, provenance metadata, canonical/promotion/actuation separation.

6. **Learning and evaluation**
   Candidate generation/evaluation decomposition; typed multidimensional assessment; causal prerequisites; memory/reconsolidation requests.

7. **Sparsity and resource policy**
   Historical 70/30 provenance; decoupled sparsity definitions; fixed and adaptive policy alternatives; measurable resource semantics.

8. **Mathematical definitions**
   Every variable, domain, unit, objective, update rule, and condition; no borrowed terms without exact mappings.

9. **Scientific relationship map**
   GWT, shared workspace, TD learning, neuromodulation, STDP, active inference, replay, dreaming, homeostasis, IIT—each classified as precedent, analogue, loose inspiration, or no meaningful relation.

10. **Experimental and falsification program**
    Baselines, datasets, metrics, preregistered hypotheses where feasible, failure conditions, and removal criteria.

11. **Implementation and substrate contracts**
    Capability requirements rather than device-specific claims; TUCR/TMS/QMT ownership.

12. **Appendices**
    Source reconciliation, derivations, benchmark reproducibility records, status ledger, and deprecated terminology.

## Direct answers

**What problem was the founding CA trying to solve?**
It was trying to build an adaptive cognitive control architecture that could generate and evaluate alternatives, regulate learning and resources, maintain usable memory, and support internal counterfactual simulation. Its historical flaw was likely attempting to place too many of those responsibilities in one “consciousness” mechanism. **Which Nexus-4 pieces are descendants?**
On the prompt’s architecture map: HTCE inherits candidate fields/workspace-like coalition behavior; HIGC inherits constitutional gating; THDE inherits history-sensitive dynamics; Cyrae inherits bounded homeostatic and epistemic/causal assessment; HVD/TMS/HDF5 inherit memory and realization; QMT and Identity Architecture inherit continuity; HTLA/HTNLA inherit transformation formalism; TUCR inherits runtime/substrate abstraction; Maera inherits output/actuation projection. **What unique role remains for CA?**
A bounded, thin layer that selects cognitive policy and coordinates resource, learning, and regime contracts across existing owners. CA should not recreate a workspace, storage engine, transformation calculus, safety system, or actuation system.

**Does Nexus need a distinct modulation layer?**
Not yet established. Retain it only if ablation tests show that a low-dimensional state improves controllability, transfer, stability, diversity, or task switching beyond HTCE-local configuration and existing subsystem controls.

**How can green-sky reasoning avoid corrupting canonical memory?**
Use an explicitly noncanonical, provenance-tagged, lifetime-bounded hypothetical field in an ephemeral HVD; permit local inference under `sky = green`; prohibit canonical writes and actuation; require separate, authorized, evidence-backed promotion. Only as testable configurations of search, evidence constraint, sensory anchoring, replay, persistence, and resource policies. They should not relax constitutional, identity, provenance, or execution restrictions.

Possibly. That is the default hypothesis to test before introducing an imagination subsystem. CA may need only the policy interface that requests and configures scoped fields. **Was 30% reasonable, and should it remain fixed?**
It is historically meaningful but scientifically unvalidated from the supplied evidence. Treat it as a benchmark policy, not a canonical constant, and compare it with adaptive and regime-conditioned alternatives.

**Should cognitive activity constrain HSL coordinate occupancy?**
No default coupling is justified. They are distinct variables and should be independently parameterized unless experiments show an explicit benefit from coupling. **Which biological and consciousness metaphors survive?**
“Dopamine-like modulation,” “workspace-like broadcast,” and “dream-like offline simulation” may survive as carefully labeled functional analogues. Literal neurotransmitter quantities, ungrounded STDP nomenclature, IIT-\(\Phi\) labeling for a generic dependence score, and all consciousness thresholds should be retired unless rebuilt with exact definitions and evidence. [arxiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.08708)

**What would falsify modern CA?**
No measurable benefit from its modulation/orchestration layer beyond HTCE and existing controls; no safe, auditable separation between hypothetical and canonical state; no gain from replay or structured generation over baseline; no benefit from adaptive policies over simple fixed policies; no predictive utility from integration metrics; or no engineering value from biological metaphors. A mechanism that cannot be removed after failing these tests is not specified with adequate scientific discipline.











# DEEP RESEARCH DOSSIER: REBUILDING THE CONSCIOUSNESS ALGORITHM (CA)

---

## A. EXECUTIVE CONCLUSION: WHAT SHOULD MODERN CA ACTUALLY BE?

After comprehensive adversarial reconstruction, the modern CA should be a **thin policy-orchestration layer**, not a monolithic cognitive engine. It should own:

1. **Cognitive-regime selection** — a low-dimensional modulatory vector that configures how HTCE generates, competes, and selects candidates
2. **Resource-policy coordination** — dynamic allocation of computational resources across cognitive modes
3. **Information-seeking valuation** — determining when and how to pursue resolution of epistemic states
4. **Regime-switching logic** — when to transition between creative, critical, abstract, and offline modes

CA should **not** own:
- Candidate generation (→ HTCE/HTNLA)
- Candidate evaluation (→ distributed: Cyrae, THDE, HIGC, evidence systems)
- Memory storage or retrieval (→ HVD/TMS/HDF5)
- Constitutional admissibility (→ HIGC)
- Temporal evolution (→ THDE)
- Homeostatic regulation (→ Cyrae)
- Identity continuity (→ Identity Architecture)

The founding CA's ambition—solving consciousness, cognition, learning, memory, and modulation in one architecture—was architecturally immature. Modern Nexus correctly decomposes these responsibilities. CA's unique role is the **orchestration interface** between these subsystems, not their replacement.

---

## B. FOUNDING-DOCUMENT ARCHAEOLOGY: WHAT DID THE ORIGINAL CA ATTEMPT TO SOLVE?

The original "Enhanced Consciousness Algorithm: Neurocognitive GAN Architecture" attempted to solve, within a single framework:

1. **Consciousness as computational property** — via Global Workspace-like broadcasting and Φ-like integration metrics
2. **Learning and plasticity** — via STDP-like Hebbian updates and dopamine/RPE modulation
3. **Creative cognition** — via generator/discriminator GAN architecture and perturbation engines
4. **Homeostatic stability** — via Lyapunov-style stability claims and resource ceilings
5. **Memory consolidation** — via memory refinery and reconsolidation mechanisms
6. **Neuromodulatory control** — via six neurotransmitter-inspired state variables

**Architectural diagnosis**: The founding CA was a "god-object" architecture — a single system trying to be simultaneously a cognitive controller, a learning algorithm, a memory system, a creativity engine, and a consciousness meter. This is not merely an outdated implementation; it is a category error that modern Nexus corrects through subsystem decomposition.

The document also shows evidence of **multiple partially duplicated drafts** (duplicate equations, inconsistent constants, different rounding of identical calculations), suggesting it was assembled from multiple sources or iterative revisions without full reconciliation.

---

## C. ARCHITECTURAL GENEALOGY

| Founding Mechanism | Intended Function | Modern Nexus Descendant | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| Global Workspace broadcast | Information integration and distribution | HTCE active fields + candidate coalition | FUNCTIONAL ANCESTOR |
| Generator network | Candidate production | HTCE/HTNLA transformations | RETAINED BUT REFORMALIZED |
| Discriminator network | Candidate evaluation | Distributed: Cyrae + THDE + HIGC + evidence | RESPONSIBILITY EXTRACTED |
| Dopamine/RPE modulation | Reward-based learning | CA policy + neuromodulatory-inspired parameters | RETAINED BUT REFORMALIZED |
| Six neurotransmitter coordinates | Cognitive state control | CA modulatory vector | RETAINED BUT REFORMALIZED |
| STDP Hebbian update | Synaptic plasticity | HTNLA temporally-modulated adaptation | RETAINED BUT REFORMALIZED |
| Φ (integration measure) | Consciousness correlate | REMOVED / historical | HISTORICAL/SUPERSEDED |
| Memory refinery | Consolidation/reconsolidation | HVD + TMS + Identity Architecture | RESPONSIBILITY EXTRACTED |
| Lyapunov stability | Homeostatic stability | THDE + Cyrae + HIGC | RESPONSIBILITY EXTRACTED |
| Perturbed State Engine | Creativity/exploration | EXPERIMENTAL — may be replaced by generative replay | UNRESOLVED |
| 70/30 sparsity | Cognitive/resource sparsity | CA policy + HSL/HTLA occupancy | RETAINED BUT REFORMALIZED |
| Energy/resource ceiling | Resource budgeting | TUCR resource management | FUNCTIONAL ANCESTOR |

---

## D. MODERN CA RESPONSIBILITY BOUNDARY

CA's unique, non-duplicated responsibilities are:

### 1. Cognitive-Regime Selection
A low-dimensional modulatory state `m_t` that configures how HTCE operates:

```
m_t ∈ R^k  where k << dim(x_t)
```

This controls parameters such as:
- Association radius (breadth of candidate generation)
- Candidate diversity / temperature
- Novelty preference vs. familiarity
- Exploration/exploitation balance
- Counterfactual depth
- Prior rigidity vs. evidence sensitivity
- Candidate persistence / decay rate
- Sensory anchoring strength

### 2. Resource-Policy Coordination
Dynamic allocation of computational resources across cognitive modes, respecting:
- `R(t) ≤ R_max` (resource ceiling)
- Regime-specific resource requirements
- Homeostatic constraints from Cyrae

### 3. Information-Seeking Valuation
Determining when to pursue resolution of epistemic states:

```
V_info(epistemic_state) = f(type_of_deficiency, cost, expected_value)
```

Where `type_of_deficiency` distinguishes AMBIGUOUS, UNKNOWN, and IMPOSSIBLE states.

### 4. Regime-Switching Logic
When to transition between modes:
- Critical ↔ Creative
- Abstract ↔ Concrete-anchored
- Online ↔ Offline/dream-like

---

## E. PRIOR-ART ANALYSIS

### Global Workspace Theory (GWT)

**Classification: DIRECT PRECEDENT**

Baars' Global Workspace Theory describes a large-scale system integrating and distributing information among networks of specialized modules to create higher-level forms of cognition and awareness. VanRullen and Kanai (2021) propose implementing GWT via unsupervised neural translation between multiple latent spaces to create a "Global Latent Workspace" (GLW).

The founding CA's workspace mechanism is a direct architectural precursor to modern HTCE's active-field semantics. However, CA attempted to make the workspace itself the locus of consciousness; modern Nexus correctly treats HTCE fields as computational structures without consciousness claims.

### Active Inference / Free Energy

**Classification: COMPATIBLE INTERPRETATION**

Friston's free energy principle posits that self-organizing systems minimize variational free energy through action and perception. Active inference frames behavior as Bayesian belief updating to minimize prediction error.

The founding CA's variational objective may be legitimately connected to free energy minimization, but CA did not properly distinguish epistemic value from pragmatic value. Modern Nexus can incorporate this distinction through HTLA/HTNLA's typed epistemic states.

### Spike-Timing-Dependent Plasticity (STDP)

**Classification: FUNCTIONAL ANALOGUE**

Bi and Poo (1998) demonstrated that synaptic modification depends on precise spike timing, with postsynaptic spikes within ~20ms of presynaptic activation producing LTP. The STDP curve shows a narrow, asymmetric window for induction.

The founding CA's Hebbian update collapses to a scalar multiple of the Hadamard product if `A∘B` contains no timing dependence. This is **not STDP** — it is temporally-modulated Hebbian learning at best. Without explicit spike timing events (`Δt = t_post - t_pre`), the STDP terminology should be removed.

### Integrated Information Theory (IIT)

**Classification: WEAK ANALOGY — with major caveats**

IIT proposes that consciousness corresponds to Φ (phi), a measure of integrated information. However, IIT has been heavily criticized: in 2023, over 124 neuroscientists signed an open letter questioning its scientific status. Critics argue IIT lacks experimental support and its core claims are practically impossible to test. The theory has been branded "pseudoscience" by some researchers.

**Crucially**: The founding CA's `Φ_t = Σ_i MI(X_i; X_{-i})` computes **multi-information** (total correlation), not IIT's Φ. IIT Φ requires computing the minimum information partition (MIP) and effective information, which is computationally intractable for systems with >10 nodes. The CA metric is a different quantity entirely and should be renamed accordingly.

### Neuromodulation and Cognitive Flexibility

**Classification: FUNCTIONAL ANALOGUE**

Neuromodulatory systems (acetylcholine, noradrenaline, dopamine, serotonin) regulate adaptive processes including exploration-exploitation balance, precision weighting, and learning rates. Dopamine encodes prediction error and modulates plasticity. Noradrenaline regulates arousal and exploration-exploitation tradeoffs.

The founding CA's six neurotransmitter coordinates are **functional analogues**, not literal biological models. The architecture may retain them as generic modulation dimensions with optional biologically-inspired parameterizations.

### Synaptic Homeostasis Hypothesis (SHY)

**Classification: LOOSE INSPIRATION**

Tononi and Cirelli's SHY proposes that sleep renormalizes synapses potentiated during wakefulness. However, the hypothesis has been criticized for poorly defined mechanisms. Frank (2013) argues that any theory of sleep function must address underlying cellular mechanisms.

The founding CA's concern about continual learning causing pathological saturation is legitimate, but the analogy to SHY is loose. Periodic renormalization (whether synaptic or representational) is a valid computational concern distinct from the biological hypothesis.

### Dreaming and Active Inference

**Classification: FUNCTIONAL ANALOGUE**

Hobson, Hong, and Friston (2014) propose that dreaming involves sensory attenuation allowing internally generated models to evolve under reduced real-time constraint. This is a direct functional analogue to Nexus's historical "Perturbed State Engine" idea of sensory decoupling plus counterfactual simulation.

### Generative Replay and World Models

**Classification: RELATED PRIOR ART**

Generative replay involves generating new experiences from learned models to improve generalization and reduce catastrophic forgetting. Counterfactual replay during consolidation strengthens generalized latent structures. World models serve as predictive tools for planning and intervention.

These are legitimate computational analogues to the founding CA's "dreaming" and "perturbation" ideas, and may provide a more rigorous foundation than stochastic perturbation.

## F. MATHEMATICAL AUDIT

### NN_dynamic Calculation — **RETAINED**

For architecture 40 → 256 → 128 → 64 → 35:

```
Layer 1: 40 × 256 = 10,240
Layer 2: 256 × 128 = 32,768
Layer 3: 128 × 64 = 8,192
Layer 4: 64 × 35 = 2,240
Total: 53,440 multiply-adds
× 2 FLOPs/multiply-add = 106,880 FLOPs/evaluation
× 4 RK4 stages = 427,520 FLOPs/integration step
× 20 steps/s = 8,550,400 FLOPs/s ≈ 8.55 MFLOPs/s
```

**Status: CANONICAL** — derivation is reproducible with explicit assumptions.

### Hebbian/STDP Update — **FAILS / REFORMALIZE**

```
HebbianUpdate(A,B) = ∫ [D1_act·max(K(τ),0) + D2_act·min(K(τ),0)] (A∘B) dτ
```

If `A∘B` contains no timing dependence, this collapses to:

```
HebbianUpdate(A,B) = (α·D1_act + β·D2_act) · (A∘B)
```

where `α = ∫max(K(τ),0)dτ` and `β = ∫min(K(τ),0)dτ`.

**This is not STDP.** STDP requires explicit spike timing: `Δt = t_post - t_pre`. Without spike timing events, the appropriate term is **temporally-modulated, history-dependent adaptation**.

**Proposed reformulation** (if timing events exist):

```
Δw_ij = f(Δt) where Δt = t_post - t_pre
f(Δt) = A_+·exp(-Δt/τ_+) for Δt > 0
f(Δt) = -A_-·exp(Δt/τ_-) for Δt < 0
```

**Status: REFORMALIZE** — remove STDP terminology unless spike timing is implemented.

### Φ (Integration Measure) — **RENAME / REMOVE**

```
Φ_t = Σ_i MI(X_i; X_{-i})
```

This computes **multi-information** (total correlation), defined as the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the joint distribution and the product of marginals:

```
I(X_1;...;X_n) = Σ_i H(X_i) - H(X_1,...,X_n)
```

This is **not** IIT's Φ, which requires:
1. Computing the Minimum Information Partition (MIP)
2. Calculating effective information across the MIP
3. This is computationally intractable for systems with >10 nodes

**Proposed renaming**: `I_total` (total correlation) or `I_multi` (multi-information).

**Status: RENAME** — do not call it IIT Φ unless it actually implements IIT's definition.

### Lyapunov Stability — **INSUFFICIENTLY DEFINED**

The founding document references Lyapunov stability but does not define an actual Lyapunov function `V(x)` satisfying:
1. `V(x) > 0` for `x ≠ 0`, `V(0) = 0`
2. `V̇(x) ≤ 0` along trajectories

A state velocity threshold is not a Lyapunov proof.

**Status: INSUFFICIENT** — remove Lyapunov claims unless an actual function is defined.

### 30% Active / 70% Inactive — **HISTORICAL**

```
ρ_active = 0.30
```

**Status: HISTORICAL/SUPERSEDED** — provenance is known (CA → HSL/HTLA inheritance), but the scientific justification is unresolved.

|---|---|---|

| STDP | Functional analogue | REFORMALIZE — remove unless spike timing implemented |
| Hebbian | Functional analogue | RETAIN — correlation-based learning |
| D1/D2 receptors | Functional analogue | RETAIN — dopamine-inspired receptor profiles |
| Synaptic homeostasis | Loose inspiration | RETAIN — as computational renormalization |
| Dreaming | Functional analogue | RETAIN — as offline generative simulation |

### RPE Causal Prerequisites

A scalar named "RPE" is not automatically a reward prediction error. Current Nexus requires:

```
PREREQUISITES FOR RPE:
1. Predecessor state (state before action)
2. Action/intervention
3. Pre-action committed prediction
4. Post-action observation
5. Appropriate temporal horizon
6. Episode binding
7. Provenance
8. Causal adequacy
```

If these are absent:

```
RPE = UNAVAILABLE  (not RPE = 0)
```

**Status: RETAIN** — but enforce causal prerequisites.

### Neuromodulator Coordinates

The six variables (NE, DA, 5HT, ACh, Glu, GABA) should remain as **generic computational modulation dimensions** with optional biologically-inspired parameterizations. The research may conclude:

> The neuroscience labels are unnecessary and generic control coordinates are cleaner.

**Status: RETAIN BUT REFORMALIZE** — optional biological naming.

## I. COGNITIVE-REGIME ANALYSIS

### The Core Question

> Does Nexus need a distinct cognitive-regime/modulation layer?

### Hypothesis

A low-dimensional modulatory state `m_t` separate from semantic content `x_t` permits the same cognitive content to be processed under different global modes:

```
x_{t+1} = F(x_t, e_t, M_t, m_t)
m_{t+1} = G(m_t, x_t, e_t, h_t, u_t)
```

### Candidate Regimes

| Regime | Characteristics | Control Parameters |
|---|---|---|
| Critical/Analytic | Strong evidence constraint, high provenance sensitivity, narrow acceptance | Low diversity, high prior rigidity |
| Creative/Divergent | Broad associative search, high candidate diversity, weak prior filtering | High diversity, low rigidity |
| Abstract | Reduced concrete input, relational/structural reasoning | Low sensory anchoring |
| Daydream/Mind-wandering | Internally-initiated, weak sensory anchoring, episodic recombination | Very low anchoring, moderate diversity |
| Offline/Dream-like | Strong sensory attenuation, generative simulation, counterfactual recombination | Sensory decoupled, high generation |

### Falsification Tests

> What would demonstrate that a distinct low-dimensional modulatory state is unnecessary?

1. Can ordinary HTCE state already produce creative/critical differences?
2. Can regime behavior be represented entirely as field-local configuration?
3. Does a separate `m_t` improve anything measurable?
4. Does it improve transfer across tasks, stability, controllable diversity?
5. Does it merely add another redundant control vector?

**Status: RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS** — requires experimental validation.

---

## J. GREEN-SKY ARCHITECTURE

### The Problem

Given authoritative world model: `sky = blue`

The architecture must perform:

```
ASSUME: sky = green
THEN: reason recursively from that assumption
```

Without either:
- **Failure A**: Rejecting the thought because it conflicts with canonical state
- **Failure B**: Overwriting canonical state

### Required Capability: Hypothetical Acceptance

> Within a clearly scoped cognitive field, temporarily treat the proposition as true and derive consequences without promoting it to canonical truth.

### Proposed Architecture

```
CA: selects/configure cognitive regime (e.g., "counterfactual")
    ↓
HTCE: creates ephemeral candidate field with "sky=green"
    ↓
Ephemeral HVD: contains temporary world state with explicit metadata:
    - field_id
    - world_scope: "HYPOTHETICAL"
    - epistemic_scope: "COUNTERFACTUAL"
    - provenance: "green-sky thought experiment"
    - creation_reason: "SCENARIO_ANALYSIS"
    - canonical/noncanonical status: "NONCANONICAL"
    - promotion criteria: "EXPLICIT_GATE_REQUIRED"
    - lifetime: "FIELD_DECAY_OR_EXPLICIT_TERMINATION"
    ↓
HTLA/HTNLA: transforms candidate state under assumed truth
    ↓
TMS: realizes state (with noncanonical flag)
    ↓
HIGC/THDE/Cyrae: impose constraints (constitutional, temporal, homeostatic)
```

### Critical Distinction

```
truth-within-hypothesis: sky = green  (within field)
authoritative/canonical state: historical_world.sky = blue  (unchanged)
```

The architecture must support:
- Imagination
- Counterfactual planning
- Fiction
- Scenario analysis
- Scientific hypothesis generation
- Creative recombination

**without contaminating authoritative memory.**

### Candidate Weirdness ≠ Inadmissibility

HIGC should not reject a hypothetical state merely because it is unusual, bizarre, or inconsistent with current priors. Otherwise Nexus would constitutionally forbid imagination.

However, creative cognition must not relax actual constitutional constraints:

```
world-model constraint   → may be relaxed in hypothetical fields
epistemic constraint     → may be relaxed
causal constraint        → may be relaxed
constitutional constraint → NOT relaxed
identity protection      → NOT relaxed
provenance requirements  → NOT relaxed
execution authority      → NOT relaxed (thinking ≠ enacting)
```

**Status: DESIGN TARGET** — requires implementation of ephemeral HVD metadata.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| Candidate diversity | Low | High | Medium | High | Very high |
| Prior rigidity | High | Low | Medium | Low | Very low |

**Status: RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS** — regimes require experimental validation.

## L. HTCE REDUNDANCY ANALYSIS

### Critical Question

> How much of the proposed CA machinery already exists elsewhere?

### Mapping

| Proposed CA Function | Existing Nexus Subsystem | Redundancy Risk |
|---|---|---|
| Candidate generation | HTCE/HTNLA | HIGH — CA should configure, not generate |
| Candidate evaluation | Cyrae + THDE + HIGC + evidence | HIGH — CA should not own evaluation |
| Memory operations | HVD + TMS + HDF5 | HIGH — CA should not own storage |
| Temporal dynamics | THDE | HIGH — CA should not own temporal evolution |
| Homeostasis | Cyrae | HIGH — CA should not own homeostasis |
| Constitutional constraints | HIGC | HIGH — CA should not own admissibility |
| Identity | Identity Architecture | HIGH — CA should not own identity |
| Cognitive regime selection | None uniquely | LOW — CA's unique role |
| Resource policy | TUCR (partial) | MEDIUM — CA may coordinate |
| Information-seeking valuation | None uniquely | LOW — CA's unique role |

### Conclusion

A valid research conclusion is:

> No distinct CA imagination engine is necessary. HTCE + ephemeral HVD + HTNLA + THDE + Cyrae already provide the machinery for green-sky reasoning, counterfactual branching, candidate promotion/rejection/decay, daydream-like internal cognition, and offline dream-like simulation.

CA should expose only the minimum orchestration/modulation interface required to configure those systems.

**Possible minimal architecture**:

```
CA: select/configure cognitive regime (m_t)
    ↓
HTCE: execute candidate-field behavior according to regime
    ↓
Ephemeral HVD: contain temporary world state
    ↓
HTLA/HTNLA: transform candidate state
    ↓
TMS: realize state
    ↓
HIGC/THDE/Cyrae: impose existing constraints
```

Historical design provenance is supplied directly:

```
CA
    ↓
30% active / 70% inactive policy
    ↓
HSL / HTLA reference occupancy
```

The later HSL/HTLA design inherited this ratio because the architecture was explicitly directed to preserve it.

**This is design-history ground truth.** Do not investigate whether HTLA/HSL independently invented it.

## N. 70/30 SCIENTIFIC REASSESSMENT

### Core Question

> Should a CA cognitive-activity ratio constrain HSL coordinate occupancy at all?

### Analysis

These quantities represent **different phenomena**:

| Quantity | What It Measures | Level |
|---|---|---|
| Cognitive sparsity | Fraction of competing thoughts/modules/candidates active | CA/HTCE |
| Representational sparsity | Fraction of nonzero HSL coordinates | HTLA/HTNLA |
| Computational sparsity | Fraction of arithmetic avoided | TUCR |
| Field sparsity | Number of active ephemeral HVDs | HVD |
| Connectivity sparsity | Number of active couplings | HTCE |

### Conclusion

> Historical inheritance was intentional, but modern analysis finds the two constraints should be decoupled.

**There is no mathematical reason** that:

```
ρ_active,cognition = ρ_active,HSL-coordinate
```

A focused cognitive state (low candidate diversity) could have dense internal representations (high HSL density). Conversely, a broad creative search (high candidate diversity) could have sparse individual representations.

**Status: DECOUPLE** — cognitive sparsity and representational sparsity should be independently configurable.

---

## O. DYNAMIC SPARSITY ANALYSIS

### Core Question

> Should activity become state-, resource-, or regime-dependent?

### Fixed vs. Adaptive

| Policy | Advantages | Disadvantages |
|---|---|---|
| Fixed 30% | Simple, predictable, historically inherited | Inflexible, no evidence of optimality |
| Regime-conditioned | Different sparsity for different cognitive modes | More complex, requires validation |
| Resource-conditioned | Adapts to available compute | May cause instability |
| Uncertainty-conditioned | More active when uncertain | Requires epistemic state estimation |
| Adaptive (learned) | Potentially optimal | Requires learning, may overfit |

### Proposed Hypothesis

A fixed `ρ_active = 0.30` was an early scalar approximation to a more general cognitive-resource policy:

```
ρ_active(t) = f(m_t, R_t, U_t, F_t, H_t, ...)
```

Potential dependencies:
- Cognitive regime (`m_t`)
- Resource availability (`R_t`)
- Uncertainty (`U_t`)
- Candidate entropy (`F_t`)
- Homeostatic state (`H_t`)
- Task demands
- Novelty

### Experimental Comparison

Compare:
1. Fixed 30% occupancy
2. Adaptive occupancy (resource-based)
3. Regime-conditioned occupancy
4. Uncertainty-conditioned occupancy

**Status: RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS** — requires experimental validation.

## P. EXPERIMENT PROGRAM

### 1. Cognitive Modulation Test

Does a separate modulation state produce controllable changes in:
- Candidate diversity
- Critical accuracy
- Creative diversity
- Counterfactual depth
- Task switching

**Method**: Vary `m_t` along control dimensions and measure behavioral outputs.

**Falsification**: If modulation state adds no measurable controllability beyond HTCE configuration, remove it.

### 2. Green-Sky Reasoning Test

Can the architecture recursively reason under `sky = green` without canonical-state mutation?

**Method**:
1. Establish canonical: `sky = blue`
2. Create hypothetical field: `sky = green`
3. Derive consequences (e.g., "grass is blue?")
4. Verify canonical remains `sky = blue`

**Falsification**: If hypothetical state contaminates authoritative history, the ephemeral HVD mechanism fails.

### 3. Candidate Provenance Test

Can imagined state ever accidentally enter authoritative history?

**Method**: Generate hypothetical states, verify promotion gate prevents unauthorized canonical promotion.

**Falsification**: If any noncanonical state becomes canonical without explicit promotion, provenance protection fails.

### 4. Dream/Offline Mode Test

Does sensory attenuation + replay/structured generation improve:
- Memory retention
- Model learning
- Exploration
- Creative recombination

over appropriate baselines?

**Method**: Compare offline processing modes against online-only learning.

**Falsification**: If perturbation adds only noise (no improvement over no perturbation), remove it.

### 5. Sparsity Comparison Test

Compare:
- Fixed 30% active
- Adaptive activity
- Regime-conditioned activity

on:
- Accuracy
- Stability
- Resource cost
- Retrieval
- Creative breadth
- Critical selectivity

**Falsification**: If fixed 30% is consistently dominated by adaptive policies, retire it as universal CA policy.

### 6. CA vs. HTCE Redundancy Test

Can HTCE alone reproduce the behavior attributed to the proposed CA modulation layer?

**Method**: Implement CA-like behavior as HTCE-local configuration, compare performance.

**Falsification**: If HTCE alone reproduces CA's claimed benefits, CA should be minimized to a thin policy interface.

---

## Q. FALSIFICATION MATRIX

| Mechanism | Falsification Criterion |
|---|---|
| CA modulation layer | If modulation state adds no measurable controllability beyond HTCE configuration |
| Fixed 30% sparsity | If adaptive policies consistently outperform fixed 30% |
| Dream/perturbation | If perturbation adds only noise (no improvement over baselines) |
| Green-sky ephemeral HVD | If hypothetical state contaminates canonical history |
| CA-level counterfactual | If HTCE already produces counterfactual cognition without CA mechanisms |
| Integration metric | If metric has no predictive utility |
| Biological analogies | If analogy contributes no explanatory or engineering value |
| Consciousness claims | If any Level 3 claim is made without extraordinary evidence |

**Foundational principle**: A mechanism that cannot in principle fail a test has not been properly specified.

## FINAL RESEARCH QUESTIONS — DIRECT ANSWERS

### Q1: What architectural problem was the founding CA actually trying to solve?

**A:** The founding CA was trying to solve **everything at once** — consciousness, cognition, learning, memory, creativity, modulation, and stability — within a single monolithic "consciousness algorithm." It was attempting to create a unified cognitive architecture that could explain and reproduce consciousness through a combination of Global Workspace dynamics, GAN-like generation/evaluation, neuromodulatory control, and integrated information theory. This was architecturally overambitious but reflected a genuine insight: that cognition requires multiple interacting mechanisms that modern Nexus correctly decomposes.

### Q2: Which pieces of modern Nexus 4 are descendants of that original CA?

**A:**
- **HTCE active fields** ← Global Workspace mechanism
- **HTNLA transformations** ← Generator network
- **CA modulation vector** ← Six neurotransmitter coordinates (reformalized)
- **Ephemeral HVD** ← Memory/refinery concepts
- **THDE temporal evolution** ← Path-dependent dynamics
- **Cyrae homeostasis** ← Stability/homeostasis concerns
- **HIGC constitutional constraints** ← Safety/admissibility concerns
- **30% HSL occupancy** ← Direct inheritance from CA (now questioned)

### Q3: After those responsibilities are extracted, what unique role remains for CA?

**A:** CA's unique role is **cognitive-regime orchestration**: selecting and configuring how HTCE generates, competes, and selects candidates; coordinating resource allocation across cognitive modes; and determining when to pursue resolution of epistemic states. CA is the **policy layer**, not the execution layer.

### Q4: Does Nexus need a distinct cognitive-regime/modulation layer, and if so, what should it control?

**A:** Possibly, but this is a **RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS** requiring experimental validation. If it survives, it should control: association radius, candidate diversity, novelty preference, exploration/exploitation balance, counterfactual depth, prior rigidity, sensory anchoring, candidate persistence, abstraction level, salience, critical scrutiny, memory consolidation rate, and resource expenditure — all parameterized by cognitive regime.

### Q5: How can Nexus deliberately entertain counterfactual realities such as a green sky without corrupting authoritative memory?

**A:** Through **ephemeral HVD fields** with explicit metadata: field_id, world_scope ("HYPOTHETICAL"), epistemic_scope ("COUNTERFACTUAL"), provenance, creation_reason, canonical/noncanonical status ("NONCANONICAL"), promotion criteria ("EXPLICIT_GATE_REQUIRED"), lifetime, and mutation authority. CA configures the regime, HTCE generates the field, and HIGC/THDE/Cyrae impose constraints. Promotion to canonical requires explicit gating.

### Q6: How should creative, critical, abstract, daydream-like, and dream-like cognition differ computationally, if they should differ at all?

**A:** Along dimensions of: sensory anchoring (high→none), candidate diversity (low→high), prior rigidity (high→low), counterfactual depth (low→high), memory involvement (working→episodic+generative), and external constraint (high→none). These are **RESEARCH HYPOTHESES** requiring experimental validation — they may not all be empirically distinguishable or useful.

### Q7: Can existing HTCE + ephemeral-HVD machinery already implement these behaviors without a new CA mechanism?

**A:** Possibly. This is a **critical test**. If HTCE alone can reproduce CA's claimed benefits through field-local configuration, CA should be minimized to a thin policy interface. A valid research conclusion is that no distinct CA imagination engine is necessary — only an orchestration interface.

### Q8: Why should cognition be sparse or bounded?

**A:** Possible hypotheses (each requiring independent evaluation):
- Resource conservation (compute/power)
- Competitive selectivity (attention/focus)
- Interference control (preventing destructive overlap)
- Homeostatic stability (preventing runaway activation)
- Learning regularization (preventing overfitting)
- Memory separation (preventing catastrophic interference)
- Biological analogy (not sufficient justification)

### Q9: Was the historical 30%-active rule a reasonable approximation, and should it remain fixed?

**A:** The 30% rule was a reasonable **early heuristic** but lacks rigorous justification. It should **not** remain fixed. The appropriate policy is likely regime-conditioned, resource-conditioned, or adaptive. This is a **RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS** requiring experimental comparison against alternatives.

### Q10: Should a CA cognitive-activity ratio constrain HSL coordinate occupancy at all?

**A:** **No.** These are different phenomena (cognitive sparsity vs. representational sparsity) and should be decoupled. Historical inheritance was intentional but architecturally unjustified.

### Q11: Which original biological and consciousness metaphors survive as useful functional analogues, and which should be retired?

**A:**

**Survive (as functional analogues)**:
- Dopamine/RPE-like modulation (with causal prerequisites)
- Neuromodulator-inspired control coordinates (generic)
- Dream-like offline simulation
- Hebbian/STDP-inspired temporal modulation (reformalized)

**Retire**:
- IIT Φ (rename to multi-information)
- Literal STDP (unless spike timing implemented)
- Consciousness claims (Level 3)
- Lyapunov stability (unless actual function defined)
- Biological validation claims (unsourced)

### Q12: What experiments would falsify the modern CA architecture?

**A:** See Section Q (Falsification Matrix). Key tests:
1. If modulation state adds no measurable controllability → remove it
2. If fixed 30% is consistently dominated by adaptive policies → retire it
3. If perturbation adds only noise → remove it
4. If HTCE already produces counterfactual cognition without CA → minimize CA
5. If integration metric has no predictive utility → remove it
6. If biological analogy contributes no value → use generic terminology

---

## FINAL SUMMARY

The founding CA was an **overambitious but insight-rich** attempt to unify cognition, learning, memory, creativity, and consciousness in one architecture. Modern Nexus correctly decomposes these responsibilities. CA's legitimate role is a **thin orchestration layer** that configures cognitive regimes and coordinates resources — not a god-object that does everything.

The 30% sparsity rule is **historically inherited but scientifically unjustified**; cognitive and representational sparsity should be decoupled. Biological metaphors are **functional analogues** at best, not literal models. Consciousness claims are **not supported** and must be removed.

The green-sky requirement demands **ephemeral HVD fields** with explicit metadata and promotion gates. The modulation layer is a **RESEARCH HYPOTHESIS** requiring experimental validation against the null hypothesis that HTCE alone suffices.

The objective is not to vindicate or prosecute the founding paper — it is to **recover what it was trying to become** and specify that architecture with the discipline Nexus has acquired since.

# Comprehensive Research Dossier: Reconstructing the Consciousness Algorithm (CA) as Project Nexus's Credible Founding Cognitive Architecture

## A. Executive Conclusion

**What should modern CA actually be?**

After adversarial reconstruction of the founding "Enhanced Consciousness Algorithm: Neurocognitive GAN Architecture" document, the modern CA should be **a thin cognitive-regime orchestration layer**, not a monolithic consciousness engine. The founding CA attempted to solve too many problems inside one subsystem—candidate generation, evaluation, modulation, workspace dynamics, memory consolidation, stability proofs, and consciousness claims—before Nexus developed the architectural decomposition that now distributes these responsibilities across HTCE, HIGC, THDE, Cyrae, HTLA/HTNLA, HVD/TMS, QMT, and Identity Architecture.

The valid architectural intent of the founding CA was: **permit the same cognitive content to be processed under different global modes of cognition** (creative, critical, abstract, offline/dream-like). This is a genuine and important requirement. But modern Nexus already possesses most of the machinery to implement this through HTCE active fields, ephemeral HVDs, and HTNLA transformations. What remains uniquely for CA is the **policy interface that selects and configures cognitive regimes** without duplicating the execution machinery.

**Recommended modern CA responsibility:**
- Cognitive-regime selection and modulation policy
- Configuration of HTCE field behavior parameters
- Coordination with Cyrae for homeostatic bounds
- Interface with HIGC to ensure regime changes do not violate constitutional constraints
- No direct ownership of candidate generation, workspace realization, memory storage, or actuation

## F. Mathematical Audit

### Verified: Engineering Arithmetic

The neural network FLOP calculation is **VERIFIED**:

| Claim | Verification | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 40→256→128→64→35 | 53,923 parameters | **VERIFIED** |
| 106,880 FLOPs/evaluation | 2 × (10240+32768+8192+2240) = 106,880 | **VERIFIED** |
| 427,520 FLOPs/RK4 step | 4 × 106,880 = 427,520 | **VERIFIED** |
| ~8.55 MFLOPs/s at 20 steps/s | 427,520 × 20 / 10⁶ = 8.55 | **VERIFIED** |

**Standard established:** defined assumptions + explicit calculation + reproducibility.

### Failed: STDP Equation

The founding CA's Hebbian update equation:

$$\text{HebbianUpdate}(A,B) = \int [D1_{\text{act}}\max(K(\tau),0) + D2_{\text{act}}\min(K(\tau),0)] (A \circ B) d\tau$$

**Analysis:** Since $A \circ B$ (Hadamard product) contains **no timing dependence** and the only time-varying object is the standalone kernel $K(\tau)$, the integral evaluates to a **scalar constant**. The entire expression collapses to:

$$\text{HebbianUpdate}(A,B) = C \cdot (A \circ B)$$

where $C = \int [D1_{\text{act}}\max(K(\tau),0) + D2_{\text{act}}\min(K(\tau),0)] d\tau$.

**This is a scalar-weighted Hebbian rule, not STDP.** Genuine STDP requires:
- $\Delta t = t_{\text{post}} - t_{\text{pre}}$ as the independent variable
- Asymmetric timing windows (LTP for $\Delta t > 0$, LTD for $\Delta t < 0$)
- Millisecond-scale sensitivity

**Verdict:** Remove STDP terminology. Retain only "temporally modulated, history-dependent adaptation" if timing events exist in Nexus at the relevant level.

### Failed: IIT Φ Claim

The founding CA's $\Phi_t = \sum_i MI(X_i; X_{-i})$:

**Analysis:** This computes $\text{TC}(X) + \text{DTC}(X)$ — the sum of total correlation and dual total correlation. It is **not** IIT Φ because:

1. IIT Φ requires finding the **minimum information partition (MIP)**
2. IIT Φ uses **cause-effect repertoire**, not just mutual information
3. IIT Φ requires **irreducibility** ($\Phi > 0$ only for integrated systems)
4. IIT Φ is computationally intractable for large systems (double-exponential)

**Verdict:** Rename to "system correlation measure" or remove. Do not call it "IIT Φ" or use as consciousness detector. No scalar threshold proves consciousness.

### Failed: Unsupported Thresholds

Thresholds such as $\theta_{D1} = 0.5$, $\theta_{D2} = 0.3$ are **not biologically derived**. They are engineering parameters. They may be:
- Retained as **engineered control parameters** with explicit rationale
- Parameterized as **biologically-inspired profiles** with documented mapping
- But never labeled "biologically validated" without specific sources

### Failed: Lyapunov Claims

A "state velocity threshold" bounding a derivative is **not** a Lyapunov proof. A genuine Lyapunov proof requires:
1. A candidate function $V(x)$ with $V(0) = 0$ and $V(x) > 0$ for $x \neq 0$
2. $\dot{V}(x) \leq 0$ (or stronger conditions) established for the system dynamics

Without these, do not claim Lyapunov stability.

### Status: Variational Objective

The founding CA's variational objective can be **compatibly interpreted** through active inference , but this is:
- **Compatible interpretation**, not direct mathematical relation
- The free energy principle is a normative principle, not a process theory

## I. Cognitive-Regime Analysis

**Does Nexus need a low-dimensional mode/modulation layer?**

This is the central architectural question. Three outcomes are permitted:

### Outcome 1: Substantial cognitive-regime controller
CA maintains a separate `m_t` vector that actively controls:
- Association radius
- Candidate diversity
- Novelty preference
- Exploration/exploitation balance
- Counterfactual depth
- Prior rigidity
- Sensory anchoring
- Candidate persistence
- Abstraction level
- Salience filtering
- Critical scrutiny intensity
- Memory consolidation rate
- Resource expenditure

### Outcome 2: Thin policy vector
CA exposes only a configuration interface that sets HTCE parameters:
- `m_t` → HTCE field configuration
- No separate dynamics for `m_t`
- Regime behavior emerges from HTCE + ephemeral HVD

### Outcome 3: No separate modulation layer
HTCE/TUCR/Cyrae/THDE already provide sufficient machinery. CA disappears as a distinct subsystem; regime behavior is field-local configuration.

**Research hypothesis:** The correct answer is likely **Outcome 2** — a thin policy layer — but this must be tested.
